The transferable rule is to aim the held bottle body over the bowl, not the eef origin.\n\nNEVER use full task prompt `put the wine bottle in the bowl` for recovery unless deliberately testing Pi0 end-to-end behavior; in A2 it knocked both objects over.\n\n## Failure modes\n\n| symptom | root cause (A<N>) | fix |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| Release opens with `terminated=false`, bottle at near/front rim | A1: placement used bowl center as eef target without enough held-bottle offset compensation; low y correction stalled at eef y about -0.072 | Aim so the bottle body is over the cavity; move through high waypoints before descending/releasing |\n| Pi0 misses after reset, gripper remains open | A2/A3: max_chunks=8 or some repeated starts can stop before closure/lift | Retry in-place from a clean pose with same grasp-only prompt and max_chunks around 14 |\n| Bowl and bottle tip near cabinet | A2: full task prompt caused Pi0 to continue into a rogue end-to-end place/release behavior | Keep Pi0 grasp-only; never use full task prompt for this cell after a miss |\n\n## Re-localization per scene\n\nBottle: identify as the dark green wine bottle with tan cork/top. Agentview identity is reliable because it stands immediately below/near the bowl and differs from the blue cream-cheese box, plate, and stove. Sample pixels on the neck/body, not table gaps. Wrist refinement is accepted only near the agentview anchor.\n\nBowl: identify as the patterned black/white open bowl with yellow rim. Use RGB to reject the red-ring plate and gray stove burner. For the destination, sample interior floor/walls and use the cavity center; rim pixels bias the target outward. Wrist can confirm the open cavity once overhead.\n\nThis run's absolute coordinates are counter-examples only: bottle samples were near x=-0.17, y=-0.05 and bowl interior near x=-0.10, y=-0.05, but future seeds must re-back-project.\n\n## Fragility flags\n\nMost fragile step: Pi0 grasp duration. Too few chunks misses; full-task wording can cause a rogue place. Fallback is a clean in-place retry with the same short prompt and max_chunks in the 12-16 range, then immediate `set_gripper +1`.\n\nSecond fragility: held-bottle offset. If the bottle appears at the near rim, do not release unless the body is visibly inside; move high first and correct the eef so the body, not the gripper origin, crosses the bowl.\n\n## Difficulty and reliability\n\nSolved in 3 attempts. Expected single-shot rate is moderate if the grasp-only prompt and 12-16 chunk retry are used and the release is chosen by visual held-body alignment. Failures were recoverable only while the bowl stayed upright; tipped bowl plus bottle near the cabinet was unrecoverable and needed reset.\n\n## Cross-refs\n\n[[avoid-full-task-prompt-after-miss]]\n"

Bowl rim points:
- far rim (near-robot side? at row 348 = smallest row = largest x): (-0.0446, -0.0185) z=0.9502. This is the rim point at largest x = -0.0446.
- left rim: (-0.0911, +0.0379) z=0.9497 → most +y point, x=-0.0911.
- right rim: (-0.1022, -0.0671) z=0.9458 → most -y point, x=-0.1022.

For a horizontal circle: the extreme y points are at x = x_center. Left gives x_c=-0.0911, right gives x_c=-0.1022. Average x_c ≈ -0.0967. (Slight inconsistency because extreme points aren't exactly at the same x with perspective, but ok.)

y center: left y=+0.0379, right y=-0.0671 → y_c = -0.0146. Radius in y = 0.0525.
x: far rim (largest x) = -0.0446 → x_c = -0.0446 - r. With r = 0.0525 → x_c = -0.0971. ✓ Consistent with -0.0967!

So bowl center = (-0.097, -0.015), radius ≈ 0.052 (diameter 10.5cm).

Held box top center = (-0.1025, -0.0234, z=0.9985). 

So the box is offset from the bowl center by (-0.0055, -0.0084) — only 1cm! Excellent. That's well inside the 5.2cm radius.

Wait — hmm, this contradicts my eyeball estimate of 2 cm toward the near side. The precise numbers say the box is essentially centered (1cm off toward -x, -y). 

Now: box bottom = top - 0.028 ≈ 0.9705, which is 2cm above the rim (0.95). The box's horizontal footprint (5x4cm) centered in a 10.4cm-diameter bowl: the corners are at radius sqrt(2.5²+2²)=3.2cm < 5.2cm. So it can descend inside without touching the rim! Great — I can descend lower, to eef z ≈ 0.965 → box bottom 0.943, below the rim level and inside the bowl. But careful about the gripper fingertips: they might reach lower than the box bottom. Fingertips: the fingers hold the box's sides; the finger tips are probably ~1cm below the box center... Actually the fingers extend from above; the box top is above the eef origin. Wait: box top = eef z + 0.006, meaning the box's top is ABOVE the eef origin. So the fingers grip the box's sides at the level of the eef origin and the box's bottom is 2.2cm below the eef.

Fingertips: in LIBERO the gripper fingertips extend below the eef origin a bit (the wrist link is above). Typically the eef is between the fingertips. So fingertips ≈ eef z - 0.02 or so, similar to the box bottom.

Plan: descend to eef z=0.965 (Δ2.7cm), then release. Actually if the box bottom then sits at 0.943 and the bowl's interior floor is at 0.915, the box would fall 2.8cm — fine, it's inside.

Alternatively, release at the current height (box bottom 0.97, 2cm above the rim, horizontally centered) — gravity drops it 5.5cm into the bowl. Risk: it could bounce off the rim edge, but the bowl's inner surfaces slope inward, so it should settle.

Memory lesson says: lower until visibly inside the rim, then release. Let's descend a bit more to be safe: eef z = 0.97 → box bottom 0.948 ≈ rim level. Then release and the box drops 3cm into the bowl. The fingers would be at rim level near the rims? Since horizontally the fingers span ±2cm in y and the bowl's rim is at radius 5cm in y, the fingers are inside the mouth. Fine.

Let's descend to 0.968 and check for OSC stall, then release.
